     .any(|d| {\n614\t                self.source_repo\n615\t                    .as_ref()\n616\t                    .map(|repo| repo == &d.depends_on_id)\n617\t                    .unwrap_or(false)\n618\t            })\n619\t    }\n620\t\n621\t    pub fn content_hash(&self) -> String {\n622\t        use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\n623\t        let serialized = serde_json::to_string(self).unwrap_or_default();\n624\t        let hash = Sha256::digest(serialized.as_bytes());\n625\t        format!(\"{:x}\", hash)\n626\t    }\n627\t\n628\t    /// Compare two issues using sync semantics instead of raw struct equality.\n629\t    ///\n630\t    /// This ignores derived or volatile audit fields that would otherwise make\n631\t    /// semantically identical issues look different across import/export\n632\t    /// boundaries, while still comparing the full synced payload including\n633\t    /// labels, dependencies, comments, and user-visible timestamps like `due_at`.\n634\t    #[must_use]\n635\t    pub fn sync_equals(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {\n636\t        if self.id != other.id\n637\t            || self.title != other.title\n638\t            || self.description != other.description\n639\t            || self.design != other.design\n640\t            || self.acceptance_criteria != other.acceptance_criteria\n641\t            || self.notes != other.notes\n642\t            || self.status != other.status\n643\t            || self.priority != other.priority\n644\t            || self.issue_type != other.issue_type\n645\t            || self.assignee != other.assignee\n646\t            || self.owner != other.owner\n647\t            || self.estimated_minutes != other.estimated_minutes\n648\t            || self.created_by != other.created_by\n649\t            || self.closed_at != other.closed_at\n650\t            || self.close_reason != other.close_reason\n651\t            || self.closed_by_session != other.closed_by_session\n652\t            || self.due_at != other.due_at\n653\t            || self.defer_until != other.defer_until\n654\t            || self.external_ref != other.external_ref\n655\t            || self.source_system != other.source_system\n656\t            || self.source_repo != other.source_repo\n657\t            || self.deleted_at != other.deleted_at\n658\t            || self.deleted_by != other.deleted_by\n659\t            || self.delete_reason != other.delete_reason\n660\t            || self.original_type != other.original_type\n661\t            || self.compacted_at != other.compacted_at\n662\t            || self.compacted_at_commit != other.compacted_at_commit\n663\t            || self.original_size != other.original_size\n664\t            || self.sender != other.sender\n665\t            || self.ephemeral != other.ephemeral\n666\t            || self.pinned != other.pinned\n667\t            || self.is_template != other.is_template\n668\t        {\n669\t            return false;\n670\t        }\n671\t\n672\t        // Handle compaction_level serialization quirk where None == 0\n673\t        if self.compaction_level.unwrap_or(0) != other.compaction_level.unwrap_or(0) {\n674\t            return false;\n675\t        }\n676\t\n677\t        // Fast path for relations: if lengths differ, they can't be equal\n678\t        if self.dependencies.len() != other.dependencies.len()\n679\t            || self.comments.len() != other.comments.len()\n680\t        {\n681\t            return false;\n682\t        }\n683\t\n684\t        // Compare labels (order independent)\n685\t        let mut self_labels = self.labels.clone();\n686\t        self_labels.sort_unstable();\n687\t        self_labels.dedup();\n688\t        let mut other_labels = other.labels.clone();\n689\t        other_labels.sort_unstable();\n690\t        other_labels.dedup();\n691\t        if self_labels != other_labels {\n692\t            return false;\n693\t        }\n694\t\n695\t        // Compare dependencies (order independent)\n696\t        let mut self_deps = self.dependencies.clone();\n697\t        self_deps.sort_by(|left, right| {\n698\t            left.issue_id\n699\t                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n700\t                .then_with(|| left.depends_on_id.cmp(&right.depends_on_id))\n701\t                .then_with(|| left.dep_type.as_str().cmp(right.dep_type.as_str()))\n702\t                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n703\t                .then_with(|| left.created_by.cmp(&right.created_by))\n704\t                .then_with(|| left.metadata.cmp(&right.metadata))\n705\t                .then_with(|| left.thread_id.cmp(&right.thread_id))\n706\t        });\n707\t        let mut other_deps = other.dependencies.clone();\n708\t        other_deps.sort_by(|left, right| {\n709\t            left.issue_id\n710\t                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n711\t                .then_with(|| left.depends_on_id.cmp(&right.depends_on_id))\n712\t                .then_with(|| left.dep_type.as_str().cmp(right.dep_type.as_str()))\n713\t                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n714\t                .then_with(|| left.created_by.cmp(&right.created_by))\n715\t                .then_with(|| left.metadata.cmp(&right.metadata))\n716\t                .then_with(|| left.thread_id.cmp(&right.thread_id))\n717\t        });\n718\t        if self_deps != other_deps {\n719\t            return false;\n720\t        }\n721\t\n722\t        // Compare comments (order independent)\n723\t        let mut self_comments = self.comments.clone();\n724\t        self_comments.sort_by(|left, right| {\n725\t            left.issue_id\n726\t                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n727\t                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n728\t                .then_with(|| left.author.cmp(&right.author))\n729\t                .then_with(|| left.body.cmp(&right.body))\n730\t                .then_with(|| left.id.cmp(&right.id))\n731\t        });\n732\t        let mut other_comments = other.comments.clone();\n733\t        other_comments.sort_by(|left, right| {\n734\t            left.issue_id\n735\t                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n736\t                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n737\t                .then_with(|| left.author.cmp(&right.author))\n738\t                .then_with(|| left.body.cmp(&right.body))\n739\t                .then_with(|| left.id.cmp(&right.id))\n740\t        });\n741\t        if self_comments != other_comments {\n742\t            return false;\n743\t        }\n744\t\n745\t        true\n746\t    }\n747\t\n748\t    /// Check if this issue is a tombstone that has exceeded its TTL.\n749\t    #[must_use]\n750\t    pub fn is_expired_tombstone(&self, retention_days: Option<u64>) -> bool {\n751\t        if self.status != Status::Tombstone {\n752\t            return false;\n753\t        }\n754\t\n755\t        let Some(days) = retention_days else {\n756\t            return false;\n757\t        };\n758\t\n759\t        if days == 0 {\n760\t            return false;\n761\t        }\n762\t\n763\t        let Some(deleted_at) = self.deleted_at else {\n764\t            return false;\n765\t        };\n766\t\n767\t        // Clamp days to a safe maximum to avoid panic in Duration::days().\n768\t        let max_safe_days = 365_u64 * 1000;\n769\t        let days_i64 = i64::try_from(days.min(max_safe_days)).unwrap_or(365_000);\n770\t        let expiration_time = deleted_at + chrono::Duration::days(days_i64);\n771\t        Utc::now() > expiration_time\n772\t    }\n773\t}\n774\t\n775\t/// Epic completion status with child counts.\n776\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\n777\tpub struct EpicStatus {\n778\t    pub epic: Issue,\n779\t    pub total_children: usize,\n780\t    pub closed_children: usize,\n781\t    pub eligible_for_close: bool,\n782\t}\n783\t\n784\t/// Relationship between two issues.\n785\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\n786\tpub #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n810\t    pub thread_id: Option<String>,\n811\t}\n812\t\n813\t/// A comment on an issue.\n814\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize,  fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {\n        match s {\n            \"created\" => Ok(Self::Created),\n            \"updated\" => Ok(Self::Updated),\n            \"status_changed\" => Ok(Self::StatusChanged),\n            \"priority_changed\" => Ok(Self::PriorityChanged),\n            \"assignee_changed\" => Ok(Self::AssigneeChanged),\n            \"commented\" => Ok(Self::Commented),\n            \"closed\" => Ok(Self::Closed),\n            \"reopened\" => Ok(Self::Reopened),\n            \"dependency_added\" => Ok(Self::DependencyAdded),\n            \"dependency_removed\" => Ok(Self::DependencyRemoved),\n            \"label_added\" => Ok(Self::LabelAdded),\n            \"label_removed\" => Ok(Self::LabelRemoved),\n            \"compacted\" => Ok(Self::Compacted),\n            \"deleted\" => Ok(Self::Deleted),\n            \"restored\" => Ok(Self::Restored),\n            other => Ok(Self::Custom(other.to_string())),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for EventType {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        write!(f, \"{}\", self.as_str())\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Serialize for EventType {\n    fn serialize<S: Serializer>(&self, serializer: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {\n        serializer.serialize_str(self.as_str())\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for EventType {\n    fn deserialize<D: serde::Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {\n        let value = String::deserialize(deserializer)?;\n        let event_type = match value.as_str() {\n            \"created\" => Self::Created,\n            \"updated\" => Self::Updated,\n            \"status_changed\" => Self::StatusChanged,\n            \"priority_changed\" => Self::PriorityChanged,\n            \"assignee_changed\" => Self::AssigneeChanged,\n            \"commented\" => Self::Commented,\n            \"closed\" => Self::Closed,\n            \"reopened\" => Self::Reopened,\n            \"dependency_added\" => Self::DependencyAdded,\n            \"dependency_removed\" => Self::DependencyRemoved,\n            \"label_added\" => Self::LabelAdded,\n            \"label_removed\" => Self::LabelRemoved,\n            \"compacted\" => Self::Compacted,\n            \"deleted\" => Self::Deleted,\n            \"restored\" => Self::Restored,\n            _ => Self::Custom(value),\n        };\n        Ok(event_type)\n    }\n}\n\n/// The primary issue entity.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Issue {\n    /// Unique ID (e.g., \"bd-abc123\").\n    pub id: String,\n\n    /// Content hash for deduplication and sync.\n    #[serde(skip)]\n    pub content_hash: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Title (1-500 chars).\n    pub title: String,\n\n    /// Detailed description.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub description: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Technical design notes.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub design: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Acceptance criteria.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub acceptance_criteria: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Additional notes.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub notes: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Workflow status.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub status: Status,\n\n    /// Priority (0=Critical, 4=Backlog).\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub priority: Priority,\n\n    /// Issue type (bug, feature, etc.).\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub issue_type: IssueType,\n\n    /// Assigned user.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub assignee: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Issue owner.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub owner: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Estimated effort in minutes.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub estimated_minutes: Option<i32>,\n\n    /// Creation timestamp.\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n\n    /// Creator username.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub created_by: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Last update timestamp.\n    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n\n    /// Closure timestamp.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub closed_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n\n    /// Reason for closure.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub close_reason: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Session ID that closed this issue.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub closed_by_session: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Due date.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub due_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n\n    /// Defer until date.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub defer_until: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n\n    /// External reference (e.g., JIRA-123).\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub external_ref: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Source system for imported issues.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub source_system: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Source repository for multi-repo support.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub source_repo: Option<String>,\n\n    // Tombstone fields\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub deleted_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub deleted_by: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub delete_reason: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub original_type: Option<String>,\n\n    // Compaction (legacy/compat)\n    // Note: Always serialize compaction_level as integer (0 when None) for bd conformance\n    // bd's Go sql scanner cannot handle NULL for integer columns\n    #[serde(default, serialize_with = \"serialize_compaction_level\")]\n    pub compaction_level: Option<i32>,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub compacted_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub compacted_at_commit: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub original_size: Option<i32>,\n\n    // Messaging\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub sender: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"is_false\")]\n    pub ephemeral: bool,\n\n    // Context\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"is_false\")]\n    pub pinned: bool,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"is_false\")]\n    pub is_template: bool,\n\n    // Relations (for export/display, not always in DB table directly)\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Vec::is_empty\", default)]\n    pub labels: Vec<String>,\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Vec::is_empty\", default)]\n    pub dependencies: Vec<Dependency>,\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Vec::is_empty\", default)]\n    pub comments: Vec<Comment>,\n\n    // Annotations (bf-only: arbitrary key-value metadata stored in bead_annotations table)\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"BTreeMap::is_empty\")]\n    pub annotations: BTreeMap<String, String>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Issue {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: String::new(),\n            content_hash: None,\n            title: String::new(),\n            description: None,\n            design: None,\n            acceptance_criteria: None,\n            notes: None,\n            status: Status::default(),\n            priority: Priority::default(),\n            issue_type: IssueType::default(),\n            assignee: None,\n            owner: None,\n            estimated_minutes: None,\n            created_at: Utc::now(),\n            created_by: None,\n            updated_at: Utc::now(),\n            closed_at: None,\n            close_reason: None,\n            closed_by_session: None,\n            due_at: None,\n            defer_until: None,\n            external_ref: None,\n            source_system: None,\n            source_repo: None,\n            deleted_at: None,\n            deleted_by: None,\n            delete_reason: None,\n            original_type: None,\n            compaction_level: None,\n            compacted_at: None,\n            compacted_at_commit: None,\n            original_size: None,\n            sender: None,\n            ephemeral: false,\n            pinned: false,\n            is_template: false,\n            labels: Vec::new(),\n            dependencies: Vec::new(),\n            comments: Vec::new(),\n            annotations: BTreeMap::new(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Issue {\n    pub fn new(id: String, title: String, source_repo: String) -> Self {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        Issue {\n            id,\n            title,\n            source_repo: Some(source_repo),\n            created_at: now,\n            updated_at: now,\n            status: Status::default(),\n            priority: Priority::default(),\n            issue_type: IssueType::default(),\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn is_blocked(&self) -> bool {\n        self.dependencies\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|d| matches!(d.dep_type, DependencyType::Blocks))\n            .any(|d| {\n                self.source_repo\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .map(|repo| repo == &d.depends_on_id)\n                    .unwrap_or(false)\n            })\n    }\n\n    pub fn content_hash(&self) -> String {\n        use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\n        let serialized = serde_json::to_string(self).unwrap_or_default();\n        let hash = Sha256::digest(serialized.as_bytes());\n        format!(\"{:x}\", hash)\n    }\n\n    /// Compare two issues using sync semantics instead of raw struct equality.\n    ///\n    /// This ignores derived or volatile audit fields that would otherwise make\n    /// semantically identical issues look different across import/export\n    /// boundaries, while still comparing the full synced payload including\n    /// labels, dependencies, comments, and user-visible timestamps like `due_at`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn sync_equals(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {\n        if self.id != other.id\n            || self.title != other.title\n            || self.description != other.description\n            || self.design != other.design\n            || self.acceptance_criteria != other.acceptance_criteria\n            || self.notes != other.notes\n            || self.status != other.status\n            || self.priority != other.priority\n            || self.issue_type != other.issue_type\n            || self.assignee != other.assignee\n            || self.owner != other.owner\n            || self.estimated_minutes != other.estimated_minutes\n            || self.created_by != other.created_by\n            || self.closed_at != other.closed_at\n            || self.close_reason != other.close_reason\n            || self.closed_by_session != other.closed_by_session\n            || self.due_at != other.due_at\n            || self.defer_until != other.defer_until\n            || self.external_ref != other.external_ref\n            || self.source_system != other.source_system\n            || self.source_repo != other.source_repo\n            || self.deleted_at != other.deleted_at\n            || self.deleted_by != other.deleted_by\n            || self.delete_reason != other.delete_reason\n            || self.original_type != other.original_type\n            || self.compacted_at != other.compacted_at\n            || self.compacted_at_commit != other.compacted_at_commit\n            || self.original_size != other.original_size\n            || self.sender != other.sender\n            || self.ephemeral != other.ephemeral\n            || self.pinned != other.pinned\n            || self.is_template != other.is_template\n        {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Handle compaction_level serialization quirk where None == 0\n        if self.compaction_level.unwrap_or(0) != other.compaction_level.unwrap_or(0) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Fast path for relations: if lengths differ, they can't be equal\n        if self.dependencies.len() != other.dependencies.len()\n            || self.comments.len() != other.comments.len()\n        {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Compare labels (order independent)\n        let mut self_labels = self.labels.clone();\n        self_labels.sort_unstable();\n        self_labels.dedup();\n        let mut other_labels = other.labels.clone();\n        other_labels.sort_unstable();\n        other_labels.dedup();\n        if self_labels != other_labels {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Compare dependencies (order independent)\n        let mut self_deps = self.dependencies.clone();\n        self_deps.sort_by(|left, right| {\n            left.issue_id\n                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n                .then_with(|| left.depends_on_id.cmp(&right.depends_on_id))\n                .then_with(|| left.dep_type.as_str().cmp(right.dep_type.as_str()))\n                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n                .then_with(|| left.created_by.cmp(&right.created_by))\n                .then_with(|| left.metadata.cmp(&right.metadata))\n                .then_with(|| left.thread_id.cmp(&right.thread_id))\n        });\n        let mut other_deps = other.dependencies.clone();\n        other_deps.sort_by(|left, right| {\n            left.issue_id\n                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n                .then_with(|| left.depends_on_id.cmp(&right.depends_on_id))\n                .then_with(|| left.dep_type.as_str().cmp(right.dep_type.as_str()))\n                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n                .then_with(|| left.created_by.cmp(&right.created_by))\n                .then_with(|| left.metadata.cmp(&right.metadata))\n                .then_with(|| left.thread_id.cmp(&right.thread_id))\n        });\n        if self_deps != other_deps {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Compare comments (order independent)\n        let mut self_comments = self.comments.clone();\n        self_comments.sort_by(|left, right| {\n            left.issue_id\n                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n                .then_with(|| left.author.cmp(&right.author))\n                .then_with(|| left.body.cmp(&right.body))\n                .then_with(|| left.id.cmp(&right.id))\n        });\n        let mut other_comments = other.comments.clone();\n        other_comments.sort_by(|left, right| {\n            left.issue_id\n                .cmp(&right.issue_id)\n                .then_with(|| left.created_at.cmp(&right.created_at))\n                .then_with(|| left.author.cmp(&right.author))\n                .then_with(|| left.body.cmp(&right.body))\n                .then_with(|| left.id.cmp(&right.id))\n        });\n        if self_comments != other_comments {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        true\n    }\n\n    /// Check if this issue is a tombstone that has exceeded its TTL.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_expired_tombstone(&self, retention_days: Option<u64>) -> bool {\n        if self.status != Status::Tombstone {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        let Some(days) = retention_days else {\n            return false;\n        };\n\n        if days == 0 {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        let Some(deleted_at) = self.deleted_at else {\n            return false;\n        };\n\n        // Clamp days to a safe maximum to avoid panic in Duration::days().\n        let max_safe_days = 365_u64 * 1000;\n        let days_i64 = i64::try_from(days.min(max_safe_days)).unwrap_or(365_000);\n        let expiration_time = deleted_at + chrono::Duration::days(days_i64);\n        Utc::now() > expiration_time\n    }\n}\n\n/// Epic completion status with child counts.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct EpicStatus {\n    pub epic: Issue,\n    pub total_children: usize,\n    pub closed_children: usize,\n    pub eligible_for_close: bool,\n}\n\n/// Relationship between two issues.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Dependency {\n    /// The issue that has the dependency (source).\n    pub issue_id: String,\n\n    /// The issue being depended on (target).\n    pub depends_on_id: String,\n\n    /// Type of dependency.\n    #[serde(rename = \"type\")]\n    pub dep_type: DependencyType,\n\n    /// Creation timestamp.\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n\n    /// Creator.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub created_by: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Optional metadata (JSON).\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub metadata: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Thread ID for conversation linking.\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub thread_id: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// A comment on an issue.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Comment {\n    pub id: i64,\n    pub issue_id: String,\n    pub author: String,\n    #[serde(rename = \"text\")]\n    pub body: String,\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n/// An event in the issue's history (audit log).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Event {\n    pub id: i64,\n    pub issue_id: String,\n    #[serde(rename = \"type\")]\n    pub event_type: EventType,\n    pub actor: String,\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub old_value: comment: Option<String>,\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n/// Changes that can be applied to an issue (non-Serde, for internal updates).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\npub struct IssueChanges {\n    pub title: Option<String>,\n    pub description: Option<String>,\n    pub design: Option<String>,\n    pub acceptance_criteria: Option<String>,\n    pub notes: Option<String>,\n    pub status: Option<Status>,\n    pub priority: Option<i32>,\n    pub issue_type: Option<IssueType>,\n    pub assignee: Option<String>,\n    pub owner: Option<String>,\n    pub estimated_minutes: Option<i32>,\n    pub due_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n    pub defer_until: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n    pub external_ref: Option<String>,\n    pub labels: Option<Vec<String>>,\n    pub annotations: Option<BTreeMap<String, String>>,\n}\n\n/// Filter for listing issues (non-Serde, for queries).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\npub struct IssueFilter {\n    pub status: Option<Status>,\n    pub issue_type: Option<IssueType>,\n    pub assignee: Option<String>,\n    pub priority: Option<i32>,\n    pub labels: Option<Vec<String>>,\n    pub annotation: Option<(String, String)>,\n    pub limit: Option<usize>,\n    pub offset: Option<usize>,\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use chrono::TimeZone;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_issue_roundtrip_from_br_format() {\n        // This is the exact format br (beads_rust) exports to issues.jsonl\n        let json = r#\"{\n  \"id\": \"bf-1bz\",\n  \"title\": \"Port br-compatible Issue data model with identical Serde attributes (src/model.rs)\",\n  \"description\": \"Port Issue, Status, Priority, IssueType, Dependency, DependencyType, Comment, Event from beads_rust. Every field, every skip_serializing_if, every rename must match the JSONL wire format exactly. Acceptance: a br-exported issues.jsonl round-trips through serde_json with zero field loss.\",\n  \"status\": \"in_progress\",\n  \"priority\": 0,\n  \"issue_type\": \"task\",\n  \"assignee\": \"claude-code-glm-4.7-november\",\n  \"created_at\": \"2026-04-29T23:21:25.206825571Z\",\n  \"created_by\": \"coding\",\n  \"updated_at\": \"2026-04-29T23:51:34.458178699Z\",\n  \"source_repo\": \".\",\n  \"compaction_level\": 0,\n  \"original_size\": 0,\n  \"labels\": [\"deferred\", \"model\", \"phase-1\"]\n}\"#;\n\n        // Deserialize\n        let issue: Issue = assert_eq!(issue.original_size, Some(0));\n        assert_eq!(issue.labels, vec![\"deferred\", \"model\", \"phase-1\"]);\n\n        // Serialize back and verify\n        let serialized = serde_json::to_string(&issue).expect(\"Failed to serialize\");\n        let roundtrip: Issue =\n            serde_json::from_str(&serialized).expect(\"Failed to deserialize roundtrip\");\n\n        //
